Other correctness fixes: `_resolve_keys` caught `KeyError` where `mel.eval` raises `RuntimeError` (broke `step_keys(keys="auto")` in batch) and omitted `source=False` on both curve-plug queries; `snap_keys_to_frames` now moves with `option="over"` (downward snaps clamped against fractional neighbors, leaving keys off-frame); `select_keys` returned inflated counts (re-counted previously-selected keys; now delta-counts, four duplicated branches collapsed); `delete_animation_layer(merge_to_base=True)` baked the result of `-affectedLayers` (layer names) instead of `-attribute` (the layer's plugs) and could never merge; `paste_keys` crashed on list `target_time` for multi-key data, recalculated copied `fixed` tangents to `auto` even in `tangent_detail` lossless mode (now re-asserts stored tangent data via `_apply_curve_tangent_data`), and restores infinity via undoable `cmds.setInfinity` instead of an om2 edit; `get_tangent_info` crashed (`TypeError`) when no key existed at the queried time (now returns `{}`); `adjust_key_spacing(exact_gap=True)` truncated fractional offsets to int and missed moved-vs-moved collisions from the clamp-at-0; `_move_curve_keys`' 0.5-frame value-query window could capture a neighboring key on sub-frame animation (now tolerance-scaled); `parse_time_range` 'before'/'after' excluded fractional keys within one frame of current (±1 → ±0.001); `fit_playback_range` misses layered animation no more (range now read from time-based curves directly, driven-key `animCurveU*` excluded); `create_animation_layer` color failures and several silent `except: pass` sites now warn. Dead code removed: `bake_objects` legacy alias (zero callers), `parse_time_range.recursive_callback`, `untie_keyframes.absolute` (tentacle's tie/untie slot updated to only pass `absolute` when tying), unused typing imports, ~25 redundant local `import maya.cmds` shadows, degenerate `Union[str, str]` hints, stale `pm.*` docstrings, and the no-op `as_strings` params are documented as deprecated (still accepted). DRY: ignore-pattern parsing unified (`_parse_ignore_patterns`), `get_driver_animation_range`'s five copy-pasted collect blocks collapsed, `get_keyframe_times`/`move_keys_to_frame` query branches deduped, `animation_diag._collect_anim_curves` now delegates to `AnimUtils.get_anim_curves`, and `scale_keys`/`stagger_keys`/tentacle's step-tangents slot use the shared helpers. `tie_keyframes` docstring now states the om2 undo caveat. Follow-up noted: `_shot_sequencer.py`'s two hand-rolled cut-and-rekey blocks should consolidate onto `_move_curve_keys`.

- **2026-07-08 — Data-node system cleanup: production-dead `DataNodes.mirror_attr` removed; audio carrier constant now sourced from the SSoT; pipeline docs refreshed.** Part of an ecosystem-wide review of the `data_internal`/`data_export` → FBX user-property → Unity pipeline (see blendertk's same-date entry for the Blender-side hand-off fix, and unitytk's for the consumer catalog). `mirror_attr` (author on `data_internal` + Maya proxy on `data_export`) had **zero shipping callers** — its only producer, the audio manifest, migrated to a plain regenerated export channel (2026-07 taxonomy fix) and `AudioClips._drop_legacy_manifest_proxy` actively heals old scenes, which stays; removal also closes the `mtk.DataNodes` ↔ `btk.DataNodes` parity delta (Blender has no attr-proxy concept; blendertk gained `ensure_export` the same day, so the surfaces now match). `test_audio_clips_export`'s legacy-migration fixture now builds the pre-taxonomy proxy pair with raw `cmds` (more honest — reproduces what old scenes contain without depending on the retired API); `test_data_nodes`' proxy suite dropped with the mechanism. `audio_utils._audio_utils.CARRIER_NODE` is now assigned from `DataNodes.INTERNAL` instead of a duplicate `"data_internal"` literal (and `AudioUtils.detect_legacy`'s default rides the constant) — `test_audio_utils_schema`'s name assertion now guards the SSoT linkage rather than two coincidentally-equal strings. Docs: `docs/data_nodes.md` reworked to the two live mechanisms (+ a retirement note pointing at the healer), documents **why `fbx_takes` intentionally ships in the FBX** despite being realized into AnimStacks pre-export (machine-readable take record, greppable without an FBX SDK; Unity joins by clip name and never reads it), and its "adding your own metadata" recipe now links the blendertk mirror doc and unitytk's consumer catalog; `docs/shot_export_unity.md` points at `unitytk.deploy_templates` for the compile-coupled C# set. **Verified headless (mayapy + maya.standalone, fresh interpreter per chunk):** the five data-node-surface modules (`data_nodes`, `audio_clips_export`, `audio_utils_schema`, `fbx_export_preparers`, `shot_export_view`) 84/84, plus the wider change-surface sweep (`node_utils`, all `audio_*`, `smart_bake`, `fbx_utils`, `unity_bridge`, `render_opacity_export`, `lightmap_baker`, `msao_fbx_export`, `shot_manifest_audio_integration`, `scene_exporter`) green. **Pre-existing bug found and fixed along the way:** `SceneExporter.perform_export(export_dir="")`'s scene-dir fallback keyed off `sceneName` truthiness — but batch/standalone reports an UNSAVED scene as a phantom `"<project>/untitled"` path where the GUI returns `""`, so a batch export with no `export_dir` on an unsaved scene silently landed in the default project instead of aborting. The fallback now requires a type extension on the scene name (Maya can't save without one; the phantom never has one; rename-without-save keeps working — and deliberately no disk probe, since a stray file at the phantom path must not re-legitimize it: this machine actually had one). The abort test's precondition assert is now environment-true (it asserted the GUI's `""` literally); `test_scene_exporter` runs 74/74 headless AND keeps the GUI semantics. One remaining known headless-only failure, unrelated to this diff (reproduced identically with the diff reverted): `test_sequencer`'s Qt row-color tests hard-crash bare standalone (0xC0000409, no GUI — the port-runner's home turf; an offscreen QApplication doesn't help). The GUI port runner itself couldn't be used this session: launched Mayas never open their command port on this machine (fails before any test loads — pre-existing infra issue worth a look; it worked 2026-06-12).
- **2026-07-08 — `auto_instancer`'s DCC-neutral core moved down to pythontk; mayatk is now a thin adapter over the shared implementation (blendertk's port is the second consumer).** `geometry_matcher.py`'s 3-stage compare (`_are_meshes_identical_uncached`), stabilized PCA basis (`get_pca_basis`'s math + `_stabilize_axes`), eigenvalue bucketing signature, and normal-agreement gate now delegate to `ptk.PointCloud.match_clouds`/`pca_basis`/`pca_eigenvalue_signature` (the UV check rides as a lazy callback, firing exactly where it used to); `assembly_reconstructor.py`'s entire clustering pipeline (16 private methods, ~700 lines: area classes, same-material touch graph, fracture repair, GCD splits, distance-consistency assignment, orphan recovery, exemplar binding, cross-copy support gate) now delegates to `ptk.AssemblySorter` — mayatk keeps only the Maya machinery (polySeparate/polyUnite, DAG grouping, canonicalization + locked-normal restore, shading-engine keys). **Behavioral fix inherited from the extraction:** the stage-3 relative-transform matrix was composed with an inverted rotation (pca_transform maps b→a; the old code assumed a→b and only rebuilt the translation row) — masked here because the assembly flow canonicalizes first (rel = identity) and the leaf tests only exercised identity/180° rotations, where R = Rᵀ; the shared `match_clouds` transposes the linear block, so genuinely-rotated baked leaf copies now place correctly (pinned by pythontk's direction-verifying tests and blendertk's live bpy placement checks). Also from the same review pass: `are_meshes_identical_with_transform` gains an empty-cloud guard (two zero-vertex meshes now match as identity instead of crashing the KDTree query on a zero-size array — `match_clouds`' own stage-1 convention; the blendertk twin had the identical latent crash). Verified on the delegated implementation: `auto_instancer` 55/55, `instancer_ground_truth` 3/3 (scene-1 sorting precision AND recall 1.000, `[5,3,1]` shape sharing, worst world deviation unchanged), `auto_instancer_scene` 1/1, full suite 3576/3576. (See pythontk + blendertk CHANGELOGs.)
- **2026-07-08 — Packaging fix: the mayatk wheel now ships the shared `package-manager.bat` menu next to `mayapy-package-manager.bat`, so the wrapper works after a bare `pip install`.** The wrapper hands off to the interpreter-agnostic menu, resolving it as a sibling first and only then via a monorepo `..\..\..\m3trik\` fallback — but the wheel shipped only the wrapper (its `package-data` listed `*.ui/*.json/*.txt/*.md` but not `*.bat`; the wrapper survived solely via `MANIFEST.in` + implicit `include_package_data`), so a pip-installed wrapper found neither the sibling nor the (absent) `m3trik/` and aborted with "Shared package-manager.bat not found". `package-data` now includes `*.bat`, and the menu is committed as a mirror in `env_utils/` (SSoT stays `m3trik/package-manager.bat`, synced by `m3trik/scripts/sync_shared_bat.py`). `test_mayapy_package_manager.py` gains a check that the mirror exists beside the wrapper and matches the SSoT verbatim. Verified: rebuilt wheel contains both `env_utils/*.bat`, and the shipped menu runs standalone from an extracted wheel. (See m3trik CHANGELOG.)
- **2026-07-07 — `Mirror`'s Z-axis radio (`chk004`) now ships checked by default in `mirror.ui`.** Designer edit; also normalizes `verticalLayout_2`'s property order. Ported the same default to blendertk's `mirror.ui` to keep the two tools' out-of-the-box state in parity (`tentacle/docs/PARITY_SURFACE.md` regenerated clean, CI parity gate green).

- **2026-07-06 — Fix: `CoreUtils._prepare_reparent`'s childless-parent guard was comparing full-DAG-path children against a node set built from the caller's raw (possibly short-name) input — a format mismatch that, depending on which side happened to be short vs full, silently skipped creating the protective temp-null in one of the two input styles.** Surfaced by the previous entry's `combine_non_instanced` default: `AutoInstancer._combine_non_instanced` always resolves candidates to full DAG paths before calling `EditUtils.combine_objects`, and when it consumed every child of a plain (non-tagged) group, Maya's `polyUnite(..., ch=False)` deleted the now-emptied parent before `_finalize_reparent` could re-parent the combined mesh back into it — reproduced as `test_original_mesh_separated`'s `IndexError` (the test's group vanished mid-run) once the full mayatk suite ran end-to-end. Root cause: `_prepare_reparent` queried the candidate parent's `children` WITHOUT `fullPath=True` while its own `node_set` held whatever format the caller passed, so the "would this parent go empty" membership check (`children` vs `node_set`) only ever matched when both sides happened to already agree — true for `test_combine_preserves_parent_group`'s short-name-only case (by accident, not by design), false for the full-path case `_combine_non_instanced` actually uses. Fix: both sides now resolve to full paths (`node_set = set(cmds.ls(node_strs, long=True) or node_strs)`, `children` queried with `fullPath=True`) — format-agnostic regardless of what the caller passes. New regression test `test_combine_preserves_parent_group_full_paths` pins the previously-untested direction. `test_original_mesh_separated.py`'s prototype cube was also bumped to `sx=sy=sz=6` (432 triangles, above `MICRO_TRI_THRESHOLD`) — its plain 12-triangle cube was legitimately being routed to the (correct, newly-enforced) micro-mesh combine path rather than the instancing path the test's docstring says it verifies; a stale test assumption, not a second bug. **Follow-up critique found the same function only ever protected the FIRST parent it saw going empty** (`break` after the first temp-null) — a combine spanning candidates from multiple original groups (exactly what `_combine_non_instanced` does across a whole scene) left every OTHER emptied group completely unprotected; empirically reproduced (two 2-cube groups, different materials → the second group silently deleted) and fixed: the loop no longer `break`s, and since multiple temp-nulls can now coexist under different parents with the same literal name (Maya only disambiguates same-parent siblings), each is tracked by UUID (`cmds.ls(name, uuid=True)`, the same survives-rename/reparent pattern `_auto_instancer.py`'s `InstanceCandidate` already uses) so cleanup can never delete the wrong one or hit an ambiguous-name error. **Left explicitly out of scope:** `_finalize_reparent` still re-parents every result from a multi-cluster combine (`group_by_material`/`cluster_by_distance` producing >1 combined mesh) under the SAME single captured parent (the first input's) — in the reproduction above, the second group's combined mesh lands under the first group's location rather than its own (now-also-preserved) group. Fixing that requires threading a per-result origin through `combine_objects`'s return value and `@CoreUtils.reparent`'s generic (single parent, single-or-list result) contract — a real contract change touching a shared decorator also used by `macros.py`, not a targeted bug fix; flagged for a deliberate follow-up rather than designed on the fly here. Also fixed while verifying this: `run_tests.py`'s results file (`test/temp_tests/test_results.txt`) was a single fixed path shared by every `MayaTestRunner` instance regardless of Maya port — running a second, differently-ported `run_tests.py` invocation while the `--all` background suite was still in flight `unlink()`-and-rewrote the SAME file, and `wait_for_results`'s file-based fallback (which just checks for a `"SUMMARY"` marker) then reported the ORIGINAL long-running `--all` run "complete" with the SECOND, unrelated run's small result — silently corrupting the exact full-suite badge this pass needed. Now scoped per port (`test_results_<port>.txt`); `test/README.md` updated to match. Verified: `test_core_utils` 27/27, `test_macros` 53/53, `test_group_combine` 8/8 (incl. the new full-path regression test), `test_edit_utils` 37/37, `test_original_mesh_separated` 1/1, `auto_instancer` 55/55 + `test_instancer_ground_truth` 3/3 (both ground-truth scenes still exact) — 184/184, run on an isolated port to confirm the concurrency fix without re-triggering the very bug it fixes.
- **2026-07-06 — `AutoInstancer` now reproduces `final_expected_result` EXACTLY on the primary ground-truth scene — scaled-copy instancing, micro-defer to the remainder combine, and four splitting/support fixes.** The 07-05 rewrite was validated only against the alt scene; the user's acceptance target is scene 1's `final_expected_result` (5 canisters sharing ONE shape, 3 uniformly-scaled cases sharing ONE shape, all leftovers merged into a single `other` mesh), and the pipeline regressed against it. Now verified: **scene-1 sorting pair-precision AND recall 1.000** vs `expected_sorting`, output shape-sharing `[5, 3, 1]` matching the reference, and **worst world-geometry deviation 0.00013 units** across every produced point; the alt scene holds at precision 1.000 / recall 0.977 (up from 0.909 — the one-off bracket variants now group via proportional support). The fixes: **(1) scaled copies are first-class** — the three cases are uniform-scale instances of one design (every part-area ratio exactly 1.169); assembly-type support now buckets by raw topology multiset and confirms by area PROPORTIONALITY (constant ratio = rigid or scaled copy; junk chains pair at inconsistent ratios and still dissolve), and the matcher's `scale_tolerance>0` path was rewritten from per-axis whitening (which erased ALL proportion information — a flat lid whitened into the same unit cloud as a tall body and "matched" at a loose tolerance) to UNIFORM-scale RMS normalization feeding the same strict rigid verification incl. the normals gate; the found scale folds into the relative transform so Maya carries it on the instance (verified on a synthetic 0.6× baked copy; pinned by `test_scaled_copy_instances_with_transform_scale`). `scale_tolerance` defaults to a `None` sentinel: the assembly flow resolves it ON (the reference demands scaled instancing), plain leaf mode stays strict. **(2) Split-model ladder in `_split_by_count`:** full identity (topology+area class) → its repeating core (now requiring ≥2 DISTINCT core identities and a core majority — a lone case's symmetric clasp pair is a part pair, not "2 copies", and shattering it destroyed the assembly) with post-split cluster-connectivity validation (disconnected clusters = core classes from DIFFERENT copies, e.g. the big copy's clasps paired with the small copy's) → raw-topology fallback (≥2 distinct topos), which is what splits a FUSED stack of different-size copies (case_A+case_B). **(3) Micro duplicates defer to the remainder combine** when it will run: below `MICRO_TRI_THRESHOLD` an instance's draw-call overhead costs more than the merged triangles (the reference merges its repeated micro tabs into `other` rather than instancing them); combined-assembly copies are exempt (an assembly instances regardless of size), and with combining off micro duplicates still instance. Assembly PRODUCTS are also protected FROM the remainder combine — a combined copy that fails to instance (a unique variant) is a semantic unit, not remainder. **(4) pythontk `pca_transform` fixes** (see pythontk changelog): the both-sides subsampling floor that silently broke every >500-vertex match, an always-included identity candidate for degenerate (near-spherical) covariances, and a Kabsch/ICP refinement rescue that converges arbitrary-angle symmetric-axis rotations (rotated canister copies) onto exact alignment — 15° spin quantization alone can never reach a 0.001 tolerance. **Durable acceptance:** new `test/extended/test_instancer_ground_truth.py` runs both reference scenes end-to-end (sorting partition scores, final shape-sharing, world-geometry preservation; skips when the Dropbox scenes are absent). Suites: auto_instancer 55/55 (+4 new regression tests), edit_utils 37/37, pythontk 1654/1654, tentacle duplicate 11/11.
- **2026-07-05 — Slots-system audit: callback-leak, teardown, and panel-correctness fixes across the UI panels.** A six-way review of all ~48 slots classes + hosting infra. **Leaks/teardown:** the shot sequencer registered Undo/Redo/keyframe/time-change OpenMaya callbacks and a ShotStore listener with no teardown — after closing the panel, `_on_time_changed` kept firing every frame against destroyed widgets and accumulated per reopen; shots and shot_manifest leaked their (class-level, strong-ref) ShotStore listeners the same way. All three now wire `ui.destroyed → remove_callbacks` (+ `connect_cleanup` in the sequencer). **Hosting infra (`maya_ui_handler`/`maya_native_menus`, TDD'd — 11 new tests):** a native-menu populate failure now restores the user's menu mode via `finally` and never caches the empty wrapper (previously a stuck menu-set swap + a permanently cached dead menu); `_load_maya_ui` evicts the half-built window from `loaded_ui` if wrapper setup fails after registration; `get(name, reload=True)` now actually rebuilds native menus; no-arg `MayaUiHandler.instance()` skips/prunes handlers whose switchboard's C++ object is dead and prefers the newest live one; `get_menu` bails cleanly in batch Maya (no menu sets). **Panel fixes:** hierarchy_manager multi-select middle-drag reparent no longer dies mid-loop (per-item `refresh_trees` deleted the remaining `QTreeWidgetItem`s → RuntimeError + partial reparent); it now resolves all nodes up front, parents them in one "Drag Reparent" undo chunk, and refreshes once. `Snap.snap_to_grid` components actually snap now — the PyMEL-era `hasattr(obj, "getPosition")` branch was dead on `cmds.ls` strings, so components fell through to the transform path and landed off-grid (new `test_snap.py` pins vertex/edge/axis-filter behavior; edges/faces snap via their constituent vertices). shader_templates: Save Template prompts for a name (was hardcoded `test.yaml` — could only ever save once), plugins load lazily in Restore instead of freezing panel open (or killing the slots instance) when `mtoa`/`shaderFXPlugin` is missing, the template combo persists by text (`restore_by`) instead of by index over an unstable `os.listdir`, and `restore_template` is `@undoable`. workspace_map: gained its missing Header (its help text never showed), the menu "Set To Workspace" button no longer shadows the real `b001` objectName, log default DEBUG→WARNING. shot_sequencer `cmb_shot` opts out of index restore (a stale index auto-loaded the wrong shot next session). shadow_rig `b001` double-fire removed (raw connect + auto-wire). tube_rig `b003` (Bind Joint Chain) gained the `@undoable` its siblings had. game_shader progress now reports to the footer (the old adapter targeted a `progressBar` that isn't in the .ui). duplicate_radial's commit-time `regroup_copies` (the Preview `finalize_func`) was a verified silent no-op in every mode — `duplicate_radial` returned names that went stale the moment `_finalize_output` grouped the copies (pre-group `|name` world paths) or the suffix pass renamed them (return value dropped), so the `objExists` guard always tripped; and fixing the staleness alone would have armed a worse latent defect (the old loop deleted the SHARED radial group while sibling copies were still inside). Now: `_finalize_output` returns live post-group paths, the suffix pass's returned final names are kept, and `regroup_copies` unparents all copies in one call before deleting the emptied group (new `test_duplicate_radial.py`, verified headless both suffix modes). `check_cmds_syntax.py` suppresses `savePrefs` (interactive-only cmd; both call sites are legit GUI paths — the CI gate was failing on it). Known-and-left: the 5 stale `MENU_MAPPING` entries (hands-off by design; overlay fallback covers them).
- **2026-07-05 — `AutoInstancer` sorting rewrite (parts no longer land in the wrong assembly) + game-ready combining of the non-instanced remainder.** Driven by user-reported mis-sorts (suitcase clasps swapping between two stacked suitcase assemblies) and validated mechanically against the hand-sorted ground truth in `example_of_a_split_assembly_alt.ma` (102 shells, assembly types A–H + leftovers): **pair-precision went 0.405 → 1.000 with zero mis-sorted parts** (recall 0.909; both residual misses are data-honest — the H copies carry different material assignments, and two A copies contain one-off ~0.9%-larger bracket variants that could never instance anyway). The fixes, in `assembly_reconstructor.py`: **(1) components are now connected components of the SAME-material touch graph** — the old pure-touch BFS + material split afterwards left same-material cliques that were only ever bridged by a different-material part fused in one "component" with zero edges between them, which the count splitter then mangled (this one failure mode caused the A-pair fusions, the B+leftover contaminations, and the phantom leftover triples). **(2) Touch-first assignment in fused splits:** a part that physically touches exactly one cluster belongs to it — scalar distances tie/alias between stacked copies (the clasp swap); growth is deliberately un-budgeted since copies legitimately differ in how many parts register bbox contact. **(3) Part identity gained a rotation-invariant surface-area class** (`_assign_area_classes`, relative-gap clustering, not bucketing) — vertex/face counts alias different parts built the same way (a body and its lid are both 12-span cylinders), poisoning every count-based decision. **(4) Core-GCD extras path:** one-off parts chained into stacked copies (a base under stacked canisters, a pallet under stacked suitcases) no longer defeat the GCD split — the repeating core splits and the extras return as loose singles, which existing orphan recovery then pairs from their own counts. **(5) Cross-copy support gate** (`_dissolve_unsupported_groups`): a multi-part group whose part multiset never repeats has no copy to instance against — connected junk chains no longer masquerade as assemblies; they dissolve to loose parts (keep-whole shortcut: a component whose FULL multiset repeats elsewhere IS one copy and skips the count split, so internally-symmetric copies aren't halved). **(6) Single-exemplar orphan binding** with a complete-multiset requirement (was ≥2 exemplar groups). Also: `_get_material` is deterministic for multi-SG shells (sorted composite key — `listConnections` order flip-flopped groupings). **Coincident-twin normals fix (`geometry_matcher._normals_agree` + `ptk.PointCloud.pca_transform`):** CAD hard edges duplicate a position with different normals; single nearest-neighbor pairing could pick the wrong twin (measured: true twin at the same distance with dot=1.0 vs picked twin at dot=-0.46) and veto TRUE rigid copies with a phantom "flipped normal" — both gates now score each vertex by its best-agreeing candidate among the K nearest within positional tolerance (partial-flip rejection is unaffected: a genuinely flipped vertex has no agreeing twin). **Game-ready remainder combining (new, default ON):** after instancing, everything that did not become an instance (unique leftovers, one-off assemblies) is polyUnited via `EditUtils.combine_objects` — new `combine_non_instanced` / `combine_by_material` / `combine_by_distance` / `combine_distance_threshold` params on `AutoInstancer` + `auto_instance()` (all wired into the Duplicate panel's `tb002` option box), gated off automatically for `is_static=False` / `needs_individual=True`; assembly-group shells emptied by the combine are cleaned up (UUID-scoped to the run's own groups). `combine_objects` itself now honors `cluster_by_distance` without `group_by_material` (distance-only clustering). End-to-end on the ground-truth scene with pure defaults: one 102-shell combined mesh → 19 unique shapes, 28 of 36 mesh paths instanced (assembly-level `Assembly_N_combined` instances), remainder merged per material, zero stray groups. Tests: new `TestAssemblySorting` (clasp-swap, material-bridge fusion, junk-chain dissolve, pallet extras) + `TestCombineNonInstanced` (defaults, per-material merge with instances untouched, distance-only clustering, needs_individual skip); `test_photo_reconstruction`/`test_clutter_rejection`/`test_touching_assemblies` now pin the grouping contract explicitly with `combine_assemblies=False` (their old passes relied on sorting being too broken to ever combine). Suites: mayatk auto_instancer 51/51 (incl. extended), edit_utils 37/37, pythontk 1652/1652, tentacle `test_duplicate` 11/11. pythontk's `pca_transform` change is backward compatible; publish pythontk before mayatk.
- **2026-07-05 — `AutoInstancer` real-scene hardening: assemblies now combine-and-instance by default, normals are preserved end-to-end (verified on production CAD data), and five real defects found on the user's test scene are fixed.** Driven by the actual test scenes in `_tests/instance_separator` (170-shell CAD combined mesh, 70k locked normals, pre-instanced canister copies, multiple assembly types). **(1) `combine_assemblies` now defaults True** (engine, `auto_instance()`, and the Duplicate panel's `tb002` option box): the whole point of sorting parts into assemblies is instancing at assembly level — with combine off, whole-assembly copies degraded into per-part micro instances. **(2) Assembly combining actually works with multiple assembly types in one scene:** the old "core" selection required a part signature in >½ of ALL assembly groups scene-wide, which is unreachable in any mixed-type scene (5 copies of type A among 11 assemblies < 6), so combining silently no-oped; `combine_reassembled_assemblies` now clusters groups by their part-signature multiset (assembly type) and combines every copy of any type with ≥2 copies — verified producing `Assembly_N_combined → N instances` on the test scene. Also fixed a crash in the same function (remainder paths recorded before reparenting → stale `|Assembly_N|...` paths downstream) and guarded `canonicalize_leaf_meshes` against stale nodes. **(3) Locked normals survive canonicalization:** `canonicalize_transform` rotates the transform while pinning geometry via `setPoints(kWorld)`, but locked normals live in object space and got no compensation — on the CAD test scene this rotated shading by a mean **73°** with 9.4k flips (measured with the restore disabled); `_capture_locked_normals` now re-pins every locked face-vertex normal in world space after the edit (mean 0.13° with it, and **exactly 0 flips / 0 moved vertices** end-to-end on the isolated real content). **(4) Matching now verifies shading, not just positions:** point clouds cannot distinguish a symmetric shape from a shading-variant twin (a flat plate maps onto itself under a 180° flip while its normals invert). Per-vertex shading normals (averaged from `MFnMesh.getNormals()` — NOT `getVertexNormals()`, which silently ignores locked/user normals) now gate every match path: ordered/unordered fast paths via `_normals_agree`, and the robust PCA path via new optional `normals_a/normals_b/normal_threshold` params on `ptk.PointCloud.pca_transform` (candidates must align normals with zero flipped vertices; among geometric ties the best-shading rotation wins — so a legitimately 180°-flipped frozen plate still instances, with the correct rotation). Combine-mode grouping previously skipped `_match_pair` entirely (whole signature buckets accepted unverified with `relative_transform=None`) — removed; every member is verified in every mode. **(5) Already-instanced geometry is left strictly alone:** separation, canonicalization (whose point-pinning through one instance path corrupts every sibling path), and assembly baking all skip shapes with >1 parent — `polyUnite` on a shared shape destroyed the sibling instance paths (reproduced as the `Assembly_4` polyUnite failure on the test scene); such meshes pass through and remain eligible for matching. **Strategy gate:** pass 1 now converts `COMBINE`-classified groups too (`_CONVERTIBLE_STRATEGIES`) — GPU_INSTANCE's repeat/triangle thresholds are engine-side draw-call advice, not a reason to leave duplicates un-instanced in Maya; gating on them skipped whole assembly groups (typical group of 5 < `MIN_INSTANCE_GROUP_SIZE=10`) and let the leaf pass shred them into micro instances (the user-reported symptom). `needs_individual`/`KEEP_SEPARATE` still blocks conversion. **Verification:** staged per-pipeline-phase normal measurement on the real scene (position-matched min-angle-over-neighbours metric, validated against a no-op null run: max 0.001°, 0 flips); isolated real content through the full default flow: **moved=0, max 0.001°, mean 0.00001°, 0 flips**; residual deviations on the full scene trace to its deliberately coincident reference groups (`expected_sorting`/`final_expected_result` — three copies of the content stacked at identical coordinates, which bbox-touch chains into single components; degenerate input, documented not fixed). Tests: new `TestAutoInstancerNormals` (partial-flip twin rejected; flipped frozen plate instances with correct world shading; locked normals pinned through canonicalize) + `TestAutoInstancerCombineDefaults` (default pin; two-copy assembly type combines to ONE shared shape) + `test_strategy_needs_individual_skips`; `test_strategy_micro_mesh` updated to the new contract (repeated micro meshes DO instance). Suites: mayatk instancing 61/61, pythontk 1648/1648, tentacle `test_duplicate` 11/11. Registries regenerated (pythontk `pca_transform` gained optional kwargs — backward compatible; publish pythontk before mayatk).
- **2026-07-05 — Fix: `ShotStore.save`/`_migrate_legacy` (`anim_utils/shots/_shots.py`) unconditionally re-enabled the undo queue in `finally` instead of restoring whatever it was before.** Both briefly disable undo recording (`stateWithoutFlush=False`) around a quick metadata write so the write itself doesn't become a spurious top-of-stack undo entry — correct in intent, but hardcoding `finally: stateWithoutFlush=True` assumes undo was always enabled beforehand. `save()` in particular runs via `evalDeferred`, decoupled from whatever undo context was active when it was scheduled — if an outer caller had deliberately disabled undo for a bulk scripted operation still in flight when this fires, it would incorrectly re-enable undo mid-operation. No reproducing test found this (the full suite's 16 files touching `ShotStore` all pass either way — none exercise the "outer undo-disabled" case), so this is a latent-risk fix, not a proven-failure fix like the `test_auto_instancer.py` one below; applied anyway since it's a strictly safer save/restore, at zero behavioral cost in the common case, matching the pattern already used correctly elsewhere in mayatk (`core_utils/preview.py`, `_anim_utils.py::optimize_keys`).
- **2026-07-05 — Fix: `test_lightmap_baker.py`'s fake `_LineEdit._OptionBox` didn't implement `resolve_affix`, causing 9 errors (`AttributeError: '_OptionBox' object has no attribute 'resolve_affix'`) in `LightmapBakerSlots.b000`.** Root cause: `resolve_affix` is real, current uitk API (`OptionBoxManager.resolve_affix`, added 2026-07-04 in uitk's affix-picker consolidation — see that package's CHANGELOG) that `lightmap_baker.py`'s production code correctly calls, but the test file's own hand-rolled fake stand-in for `option_box` (written before that API existed) was never updated to match — a test-fixture-drifted-from-the-real-API bug, not a production bug. Fix: gave the fake `_OptionBox` a `resolve_affix(*, default)` that delegates to the exact same fallback path uitk's real implementation uses when no `AffixOption` picker is attached — `pythontk.StrUtils.split_affix(widget.text(), mode="auto", default=default)` — rather than reimplementing the parsing logic; verified against the file's own two explicit affix-mode test cases (`"_Lightmap"` → suffix, `"LM_"` → prefix). Found while investigating why a full `run_tests.py --all` run (done to restore the README badge after an unrelated scoped-run mistake, see the entries below) showed unexpected errors, and fixed per this repo's "never just leave a failing test" rule despite being unrelated to that work. Verified: `test/test_lightmap_baker.py` 41/41 (fresh live Maya, `--no-badge`).
- **2026-07-05 — Fix: `test_auto_instancer.py::test_single_undo_restores_originals` left Maya's global undo queue disabled for every test that ran after it in the same process.** Its `finally` unconditionally called `cmds.undoInfo(state=False)`, on the wrong assumption that `False` was always the pre-test baseline — works fine when the file runs alone (a fresh Maya defaults to undo enabled, so there's nothing to "leak"), but breaks the moment it runs inside `run_tests.py --all`'s single shared, long-lived Maya process alongside modules that need undo enabled. Surfaced as `RuntimeError: The undo queue is turned off.` in `test_core_utils::test_undoable_decorator` and 4 cases in `test_curve_to_tube` (both alphabetically after `auto_instancer`) during a full-suite run done to restore the README badge (see the `StyleSetter` entry below) — unrelated to that work, found and fixed along the way per this repo's "never just leave a failing test" rule. Fix: capture `cmds.undoInfo(query=True, state=True)` before the test forces it on, restore that captured value in `finally` instead of a hardcoded `False` — the standard save/restore pattern for a test that mutates shared global state. Verified: a scoped `run_tests.py auto_instancer core_utils curve_to_tube hierarchy_manager` re-run in a fresh Maya is fully green (353 tests, 0 errors) where it previously showed 7 errors across the 3 downstream modules.
- **2026-07-05 — `StyleSetter` drops its backup/restore feature entirely (`BACKUP_NAME`, `ensure_backup`, `backup_current`, `has_backup`, `backup_dir`, `backup_path`, `restore_default_style`, plus the capture-side helpers `_capture_rgb`/`_capture_display_color`/`_capture_snapshot`/`_all_style_keys` that only backed it) — parity move with blendertk, which dropped the same feature the same day (see that CHANGELOG for the fuller rationale).** `list_templates()` now returns just the shipped styles (`{"Blender": "Blender"}`, no `Default` entry); `apply_template(name)` is a plain forward to `set_style(name)` (the `if name == BACKUP_NAME` branch is gone). `set_style` no longer calls `ensure_backup()` first. The tentacle `cmb003_init` slot (`slots/maya/preferences.py`) no longer calls `ensure_backup()` or auto-selects a `Default` entry — it just populates the combo, mirroring Blender's own dropdown having no "currently selected" indicator either. **Asymmetry worth flagging:** unlike blendertk (whose combo still always has a way back — Blender's own built-in themes stay in `list_templates()` regardless of backup logic), mayatk's combo now has genuinely nothing to revert to from the UI once a style is applied, since Maya has no built-in "theme" of its own to fall back on. Out of scope for this change (removal was explicitly requested, not "remove and replace with a new revert mechanism") but real — a future ask for Maya-side revert would need a different mechanism than what was removed here, not a restoration of it. `EnvUtils.get_env_info("prefs_path")` was NOT removed despite losing its only internal caller — it sits in the same general-purpose env-info dict as several other keys (`presets_path`, `version`, `renderer`, `workspace_path`) that also have zero current internal callers, so removing just this one would be inconsistent with how that registry is maintained. Test suite rewritten: `test_backup_name_is_default` and `test_set_style_backup_and_restore_roundtrip` replaced with `test_no_backup_restore_api` (asserts every removed name is gone from both the module and `StyleSetter`) and `test_set_style_applies_full_overlay` (keeps the still-valid coverage of `set_style`'s color-overlay behavior, which the removal didn't touch); the class-level `setUpClass`/`tearDownClass` backup bookkeeping was replaced with a per-test `setUp`/`tearDown` that captures + restores just the 3 raw color keys directly via `cmds`, since the suite still runs every test against the SAME live, persistent Maya instance and needs its own hygiene now that the production module no longer provides any. Verified: `test/test_style_setter.py` 6/6 in a fresh live Maya. **Recurrence of a known gotcha:** running a single scoped module (`run_tests.py style_setter`) again overwrote `docs/README.md`'s Tests badge down to that module's own count — the exact failure mode already documented in the 2026-07-04 entry below (`update_readme_badge` writes whatever this run's total was, not the repo's real total) — required a full `--all` re-run to restore the true total, which surfaced an unrelated pre-existing bug (see the entry above this one).
- **2026-07-05 — `AutoInstancer` gains a one-shot API entry point, `auto_instance()`, and moves to its own package `core_utils/auto_instancer/` — no dedicated UI; it's exposed as a single option-box button in tentacle's existing Duplicate panel.** A standalone panel (separate Preview/Instance buttons, header Scope/Log-Level combos, in-panel log) was built and fully verified first, but decided against — this tool doesn't need its own window; it belongs alongside the Duplicate panel's other instancing tools. `auto_instance(nodes=None, tolerance=0.001, ...)` (module-level, mirrors `replace_with_instances`/`get_instances`/`uninstance`) is a thin wrapper: construct an `AutoInstancer` from plain kwargs, call `run()`, return the result — so callers (a UI slot, a script) don't need to touch the class directly. **Package rename:** `core_utils/instancing/` → `core_utils/auto_instancer/`, and its engine module `auto_instancer.py` → `_auto_instancer.py`, matching the one-package-per-tool convention already used by `naming/_naming.py` and `smart_bake/_smart_bake.py`. All internal imports, the root `mayatk/__init__.py` registry entry (now `["AutoInstancer", "auto_instance"]`), and the three affected test files (`test_auto_instancer_scene.py`, `test_instancing_extras.py`, `test_original_mesh_separated.py`) were updated; API registry regenerated. tentacle detail (Duplicate panel's new `tb002` button) is in the tentacle CHANGELOG.
- **2026-07-05 — `AutoInstancer` optimization pass: 115× on the assembly flow (32.4s → 0.28s for 30 combined canisters), rooted in a matching-correctness fix.** Profiling showed 568 of 680 robust PCA comparisons were identical parts FAILING to match: `eigh`'s eigenvectors are arbitrary within a degenerate (rotationally symmetric) eigen-subspace, so identical cylinder/sphere copies each canonicalized with a different spin — the robust matcher's 15°-grid spin search then missed on an 18°-per-segment cylinder at default tolerance, silently leaving identical parts un-instanced (the loose-tolerance tests hid it). `GeometryMatcher.get_pca_basis` now stabilizes the frame from the geometry itself (`_stabilize_axes`: third-moment sign anchoring; first-vertex anchoring for degenerate subspaces), so identical copies share canonical frames and hit the exact fast path — the perf probe's result count changed from 55 (partial failures) to the correct 30. Regression test: `test_symmetric_parts_match_at_default_tolerance`. Supporting optimizations: `GeometryMatcher` memoizes per-shape-pair results and object-space point arrays (`clear_cache()`; hierarchy retry logic re-visits the same pairs many times), the ordered fast-path compare is vectorized, candidate filtering batch-resolves locked/referenced nodes in two `ls` calls instead of two queries per node, bbox adjacency is numpy-broadcast instead of an O(n²) Python loop, and BFS uses a deque.
- **2026-07-05 — `AutoInstancer` production-hardening audit (core_utils/auto_instancer, named `core_utils/instancing` at the time — see the package-rename entry above).** Safety: whole run is one undo chunk; originals are deleted only after the replacement instance is fully assembled (per-member failure isolation — a bad member no longer aborts the run or loses geometry); locked/referenced nodes are skipped in both candidate paths; hierarchy mode no longer "instances" meshless transforms (cameras/locators/empty groups previously matched each other's empty signatures and were deleted); the second (leaf) pass is scoped to the input node set with full-path comparison (previously scanned the whole scene, and its processed-set never matched due to short/long name mixing). Correctness: `run()` no longer mutates `check_hierarchy` (re-entrant; `find_instance_groups` gained an optional `check_hierarchy` override); `_merge_similar_signatures` no longer merges buckets across differing material/UV signature components (violated `require_same_material` in combine mode); removed an unreachable translation-match block in `GeometryMatcher.are_meshes_identical`. Sorting/determinism: group processing order and prototype selection now use natural sort (`Cube2` < `Cube10`) with full-path tie-breaks; groups are ordered by their shallowest MEMBER (members are what get deleted, so ancestor replacements run before groups matching their descendants — prototype depth misordered when prototype selection picked a different depth than the members); a group whose prototype was consumed by an ancestor replacement now promotes a surviving member and re-matches the rest instead of dropping the group (stale relative transforms are recomputed); the distance-consistency vote includes adjacent quantization buckets so equal distances straddling a bucket boundary can't silently degrade to greedy assignment. Assembly reconstruction: NEW distance-consistency assignment (identical assemblies put a part at the same internal distance from its anchor in every copy — fixes the stacked-canister "chain-reaction" mis-assignment), fractured-fusion repair, and exemplar-guided orphan recovery (parts with an air gap to their own assembly no longer fracture the GCD split); removed the overfit `_cluster_by_anchors` heuristic (hardcoded 5/3/1 counts; no test scene ever triggered it) and the dead `combine_targets`; assembly groups are tagged with an `autoInstancerAssembly` attribute so user nodes named `Assembly_*` are never swept into combine; leftover empty source transforms from `polySeparate` are cleaned up. Strategy thresholds are now named class constants; debug `print`s → loggers; `scale_tolerance`/`search_radius_mult`/`combine_assemblies` setters now forward to collaborators. **API note:** `GeometryMatcher.get_mesh_signature` now returns `(verts, edges, faces, pca_sig, materials, uv_signature)` — the always-`0.0` `approx_area` slot (and the unused `include_area` param) were removed; index-based consumers of the old 7-tuple must shift indices ≥ 4 down by one. Tests: new `TestAutoInstancerProductionSafety` regression class (6 tests: meshless-hierarchy guard, locked-node skip, config immutability, natural-order prototype, single-undo restore, second-pass scoping); repaired the extended tests (PyMEL leftovers, ambiguous-name queries, unseeded random) — full suite 59/59 in mayapy, including the previously failing extended tier.
- **2026-07-04 — Refactor: `ScriptConsole` now consumes the shared `uitk.ScriptOutput`.** The syntax-highlighter + read-only output widget moved to uitk (host-agnostic, shared with blendertk); mayatk injects the Maya-specific behavior — the context-menu **Clear** also empties Maya's reporter (`clear_callback`), and the **Echo All Commands** toggle is added via `context_menu_hook`. The `workspaceControl` dockable host + live `cmdScrollFieldReporter1` mirror are unchanged. Removes the local `ScriptHighlightRule`/`ScriptHighlighter`/`ScriptOutput` classes (−172 lines) in favor of the uitk copy. **Release order:** publish uitk before mayatk (mayatk now imports the new `uitk.ScriptOutput` symbol — a stale published uitk would crash the console on the user's machine).
- **2026-07-04 — Parity: `style_setter`'s shipped-data dir renamed `colors/` → `styles/` (matches blendertk's `style_setter/styles/`).** The dir was `colors/` here and `themes/` in blendertk — a real cross-package drift (neither name fits both). Both are now `styles/`, matching the shared `StyleSetter` / `list_styles` / `set_style` vocabulary; `STYLES_DIR` replaces `COLORS_DIR` (public module const). The file *format* still legitimately differs (bespoke `.json` here — **Maya has no native named-preset format for UI colors**, confirmed again this pass: the user's Maya prefs dir has `userRGBColors2.mel` for viewport/UI colors, not XML; the `*.xml` in that dir are `synColorConfig`/`synColorFileRules`, which belong to the unrelated **color-management / rendering** pipeline, not the "Colors" preferences window — vs native `.xml` in blendertk). Ledgered in `blendertk/docs/STRUCTURE.md`: same dir, same class, same API, format divergence justified — not drift. Verified: `test/test_style_setter.py` 6/6 in live Maya; clean-build wheel (`rm -rf build/` first — setuptools doesn't prune stale `build/lib/`, see blendertk CHANGELOG) ships `styles/Blender.json` only.
- **2026-07-04 — `mayatk.StyleSetter` gains `list_templates()`/`apply_template()` and renames its backup `Default_Backup` → `Default`, to match blendertk's `StyleSetter` and drive the shared tentacle theme-selector combo.** Follow-up to the entry below. `list_templates()` returns an ordered `{display_name: token}` — the `Default` backup of the user's own colors (present once `ensure_backup()` has run) followed by each shipped style (`Blender`); `apply_template(name)` routes a `Default` token to `restore_default_style()` and any other to `set_style(name)`. This is the same public pair blendertk exposes (there `apply_template` takes a native preset filepath; here a style name — same method name + role, host-appropriate token), so tentacle's shared `preferences.ui` combo (`cmb003`) drives Maya and Blender through one branch-free slot shape. Unlike Blender, Maya has no *native* named-color-preset selector to mirror (its Colors editor offers no template dropdown), so `list_templates()` is our own set, not a host-provided one — the JSON representation stays (it's the correct choice: no native format exists to defer to, and JSON allows the dynamic grid-slot resolution a flat `.mel` snapshot couldn't). The backup constant rename flows through to the on-disk filename (`<prefs>/mayatk_style_setter/Default.json`). Verified: `test/test_style_setter.py` +2 (`Default` naming, `list_templates`/`apply_template` round-trip) = **6/6** in a fresh live Maya via `run_tests.py`; plus a live end-to-end probe drove the **real tentacle `Preferences.cmb003_init`/`cmb003`** against the real `StyleSetter` in a launched Maya (no mocks) — combo populated, `Default` auto-selected by token, `Blender` recolored the viewport bg to 0.188, `Default` reverted it exactly, backup artifact self-cleaned (6/6). That probe also caught a real bug in the tentacle slot (indexing the combo by display label instead of item-data token — see the tentacle CHANGELOG).
- **2026-07-04 — `mayatk.StyleSetter`: the mayatk counterpart to blendertk's `StyleSetter`, overlaying a "Blender" look onto Maya's scriptable viewport colors.** blendertk's `StyleSetter` (2026-07-04, blendertk CHANGELOG) reskins Blender's full UI chrome to Maya's look via Blender's native `interface_theme` preset system; asked to build "the equivalent" here, empirical research first (fresh Maya 2025 via `MayaConnection`) established there's no mayatk-side equivalent for widget chrome at all — Maya's `adskdarkflatui` is a compiled native `QStyle` with nothing scriptable/exportable. What Maya *does* expose scriptably is its "Colors" preferences: `cmds.displayRGBColor` (300+ direct RGBA colors), `cmds.colorIndex` (a 31-slot indexed palette), `cmds.displayColor` (maps an object/component-type name to a palette index, per dormant/active state — confirmed grid-family names have no "active" variant, since they aren't selectable). New `ui_utils/style_setter/` subpackage (`_style_setter.py` + shipped `colors/Blender.json`), registered as just `StyleSetter` (mirrors the class-only registration convention, not a spray of generic names into flat `mtk.*`). Deliberately narrower than blendertk's design in two ways, both because the underlying capability genuinely differs: **(1)** `set_style` never calls Maya's own bulk `rf=True` factory-reset — unlike a Blender theme (a complete, atomic replacement), Maya's 300+ colors are independent knobs, and a full reset would blank out thumbnail-unrelated colors (node editor, hypergraph, script-editor syntax, …) that have nothing to do with "the look"; it's a **targeted overlay** of only the keys a style's JSON defines (viewport background + dormant grid + dormant polygon-edge color for the shipped "Blender" style — the always-on, visually-dominant subset, chosen over the full ~60-name `displayColor` surface to stay within what was actually verified rather than guess at unconfirmed names). **(2)** No native preset file format exists on the Maya side either (confirmed via `colorPrefWnd.mel` — Colors editor only offers Save/Reset-to-saved/Reset-to-factory, all in-place on the user's own prefs file), so — unlike blendertk's native-XML redo — a bespoke JSON is the *correct* choice here, not a shortcut around a format that doesn't exist. `ensure_backup()`/`backup_current()` snapshot only the specific keys any shipped style touches (not the full palette) into `<Maya user prefs dir>/mayatk_style_setter/Default_Backup.json` (`EnvUtils.get_env_info` gained a `prefs_path` key, `cmds.internalVar(userPrefDir=True)`, alongside the existing `user_app_path`/`presets_path` entries) before the first switch; `restore_default_style()` puts them back. `persist=True` on either call writes through Maya's own `cmds.savePrefs(colors=True)`. Verified end-to-end in a fresh disposable Maya 2025 (`MayaConnection`, `force_new_instance=True`, port mode): background/grid/dormant-edge round-trip through set→verify→restore→verify exactly, backup file writes and reads back correctly, unknown-style raises `FileNotFoundError`, namespace hygiene holds (`mtk.set_style`/`mtk.list_styles` absent). Permanent suite `test/test_style_setter.py` (4 tests) added — must run via `run_tests.py`'s real interactive/GUI Maya launch, never `mayapy` standalone (`displayRGBColor` et al. raise in batch mode); 4/4 green. `blendertk/docs/STRUCTURE.md`'s `ui_utils` row updated (was "no mayatk equivalent" for the whole feature; now documents the two sides mirror name+behavior only, targeting different scriptable surfaces). Two pre-existing bugs fixed while running the new suite: `test/run_tests.py` never actually imported `os` at module scope (two `import os` lines inside code sent to a remote Maya's exec, mistaken for real imports) — `update_readme_badge` crashed with `NameError: name 'os' is not defined` at the end of *every* run, after results were already reported; fixed with a real top-level `import os`, which also self-corrected `docs/README.md`'s badge link target (`test/` → `../test/`, the actual correct relative path from `docs/`). The badge's pass-count (accidentally overwritten to "4 passed" by running just this one module in isolation to verify the fix) was restored to the last full-suite figure (397 passed) rather than left misleadingly low.
- **2026-07-04 Fix: stale hand-maintained Version badge + self-breaking Tests-badge writer.** `docs/README.md`'s static Version badge said 0.11.51 while `__version__` is 0.12.64 — nothing regenerates it, so it drifts every release; replaced with the dynamic `img.shields.io/pypi/v/mayatk` shield (the uitk pattern). Also fixed `test/run_tests.py::update_readme_badge`, which hardcoded the badge link target as `](test/)` while writing into `docs/README.md` — every badge refresh would have re-broken the corrected `../test/` link; the target is now computed relative to the README's location (same fix in the pythontk/uitk/tentacle runners).
- **2026-07-04 Docs: format specs wired into the docs hub.** The three co-located format specs (`shot_manifest` behaviors + mapping, scene-exporter `TEMPLATE_RULES`) were unreachable from any hub doc; `docs/README.md`'s Guides section now links them, and `CLAUDE.md` Nav links the docs hub. Part of the monorepo docs-standard pass.
- **2026-07-03 — Affix-mode picker migrated to the shared uitk `AffixOption`; local `mat_utils/_affix_mode.py` deleted.** The DCC-agnostic affix picker (Auto / Suffix / Prefix) was pure uitk/Qt widget wiring with zero Maya dependency and was duplicated verbatim in blendertk, so it was promoted to `uitk.AffixOption` (`widgets/optionBox/options/affix.py`). The three callers — `mat_utils/image_to_plane`, `mat_utils/game_shader`, `light_utils/lightmap_baker` — now use the option-box manager surface: `widget.option_box.set_affix(default=…, on_change=…)` for setup and `widget.option_box.affix_mode` / `.resolve_affix(default=…)` for read-back (no imports needed; `.option_box` is autopatched onto plain `QLineEdit` too, unlike the `.options` fluent sugar). `mat_utils/_affix_mode.py` (and its `add_affix_mode_menu` / `current_affix_mode` / `resolve_affix` functions) is removed — behavior is unchanged; the parsing was already delegated to `pythontk.StrUtils.split_affix`. Requires uitk ≥ the 2026-07-03 build that ships `AffixOption`; release uitk first (`push.ps1` dependency order).
- **2026-07-02 — Smart Bake panel: the whole-scene scope is now named and defaulted as "Auto", and the header menu gained a Reset to Defaults button.** The engine's `analyze()`/`bake()` already scanned every transform/joint and baked only the ones actually driven when Scope was "All Transforms & Joints" — the smart filtering was real but mislabeled, reading like a force-bake-everything option. The combo is now `["Auto (Whole Scene)", "Selected"]` with Auto as index 0 (the new default, previously Selected) — no engine behavior changed, only the label/order and the tooltip/help-text/getting-started copy, which now say outright that Auto bakes only what's driven. `header_init` adds a `Reset to Defaults` `QPushButton` (`widget.menu.add(...)`, matching the Lightmap Baker header-menu convention) wired to a new `reset_defaults()` slot calling `self.ui.state.reset_all()` (the existing uitk `StateManager` reset-all, same primitive `curtain.py`/`duplicate_grid.py` etc. use from a dedicated button). Tests: `test_smart_bake_slots.py` scope tests updated for the new index mapping (10/10 green under mayapy unittest); `mock_tests/test_smart_bake_panel.py` combo-items assertion updated (9/9 green); verified live (offscreen Qt + mocked cmds) that the header menu's `reset_defaults` button exists and the slot runs.
- **2026-07-02 — DataNodes system-wide critique pass: one carrier API, self-registering export producers, dead code deleted.** Full survey of the scene-data-node system (producers Shots/Audio/SmartBake/LightmapBaker, consumers FbxUtils/SceneExporter/unitytk) followed by a structural cleanup. **(1) Third carrier pattern eliminated:** `MayaScenePersistence` (Shots) now persists the store on the shared `data_internal` node (`shot_store` channel via `DataNodes.set_internal_string`) instead of its own dedicated `shotStore` network node; a legacy node is folded in transparently on first load (guarded by the `shotData` attr signature so an unrelated node that merely shares the name is never touched) and deleted. `NodeUtils.ensure_data_node` — whose only caller that was — is deleted along with its tests; the scene now carries exactly two metadata nodes and one API. **(2) Dead migration deleted:** `DataNodes.migrate_legacy_carriers()` had zero production callers and migrated legacy `audio_events*` carriers to the *obsolete intermediate* single-enum `audio_trigger` schema that the current per-track audio model no longer reads; `audio_utils.migrate.migrate_legacy_triggers` (UI-invoked) already handles the same carriers and lands on the current schema, so the DataNodes copy + its 6 tests are gone (docs now point at the one migration). **(3) `audio_manifest` taxonomy fix:** the manifest is a regenerated export artifact, yet was the only channel using the `mirror_attr` proxy path; `AudioClips.prepare_for_export` now writes it as a plain `set_export_string` channel on `data_export` (matching `fbx_takes`/`shot_metadata`/`lightmap_metadata`), and `_drop_legacy_manifest_proxy` self-heals pre-taxonomy scenes in place (detects the proxy via `addAttr -q usedAsProxy`, drops the pair, regression-pinned). Unity side unchanged — it reads the FBX user property either way; unitytk comments/README updated. **(4) Producer registry unified (OCP):** new `FbxUtils.run_export_preparers()` runs every registered session preparer plus a declarative `_KNOWN_PRODUCERS` fallback list — the Scene Exporter's `export_data_node` task now delegates to it instead of hardcoding Shots+Audio imports, and `_log_data_node_summary` is channel-agnostic (every user-defined string attr on the carrier summarized by entry count), so a new metadata producer ships with zero exporter edits. The before-export hook reuses the same runner (registered-only). **(5) The any-export hook is no longer inert:** `ShotStore.enable_auto_export`/`AudioClips.enable_auto_export` had *no* production caller — the documented "metadata rides into any FBX export" path never activated. Producers now self-register their preparer on authoring (saving a store with shots / creating an audio track), with a session-global explicit opt-out (`disable_auto_export` sets a sticky flag that auto-registration respects). **(6) Maya↔Blender parity:** `set_export_string("")` now clears-without-creating (blendertk's semantics; LightmapBaker's hand-rolled guard deleted), and blendertk's `get_export_string` returns `None` for a cleared key (was `""`, diverging from mayatk). `docs/data_nodes.md` rewritten (all four export channels + the five internal channels tabulated, `set_internal_string` documented, migration pointer). Tests: +1 legacy-shotStore-migration (sequencer), +1 proxy-self-heal (audio_clips_export), +6 export-string/empty-guard (data_nodes); updated: scene_exporter summary-format asserts, shot_export_view hook-teardown (authoring now legitimately holds the hook), blendertk clear-semantics assert, mock persistence patch targets. Green in fresh mayapy: data_nodes 31, node_utils 29, audio_clips_export 5, fbx_export_preparers 7, sequencer 269, shot_export_view 20, scene_exporter 74, audio_utils_phase2f 29, lightmap_baker 41, smart_bake 57; blendertk headless 21/21.

- **2026-07-02 — Smart Bake gets a dedicated tool panel (Bake + one-click Unbake), and building it surfaced a critical pre-existing engine bug: every override-layer bake wrote FLAT curves.** **The bug (fixed + regression-pinned):** `_create_override_layer` pre-registered the bake attributes onto the fresh layer (`create_animation_layer(attributes=[...])`) before `bakeResults(destinationLayer=...)` targeted it — that combination writes a flat constant (the value live at registration time) at every sampled frame instead of the true curve. Bisected live in mayapy: a locator-constrained cube animated 0→5 baked back motionless at every frame; handing `bakeResults` the **empty** layer and letting it wire the attributes itself reproduces the motion exactly (auto-tangent shape included). Impact was silent and broad: the scene exporter's FBX pass samples the evaluated scene *with the layer live*, so exports of constrained/driven objects have been flat since layer mode landed; nothing caught it because every existing test asserted values only *after* restore (constraint resumes → correct again). Bonus: the same fix keeps `ikBlend` at 1.0 through a layer bake — the "DIC kills IK even on a layer" behavior in the entry below was this same corruption (its manifest-based `ikBlend` restore is retained as base-layer insurance). New `TestLayerBakeCorrectness` pins per-frame baked-vs-driver equality *while the layer is live* plus a non-flat layer-curve check; `AnimUtils.create_animation_layer`'s `attributes` docstring now carries the CAUTION, and SmartBake is the only register-then-bake caller repo-wide (grep-confirmed). **The panel:** Smart Bake is now a dedicated submodule `anim_utils/smart_bake/` (`_smart_bake.py` engine, `bake_session.py`, `smart_bake.ui`, `smart_bake_slots.py`, docstring-only `__init__` — mirrors `blendshape_animator/`; `DEFAULT_INCLUDE` + all import sites updated), discovered by `MayaUiHandler` — launch standalone via `MayaUiHandler.instance().show("smart_bake")` or from tentacle. Follows the HDR-Manager/Lightmap-Baker composition conventions (`ptk.LoggingMixin + ptk.HelpMixin`, `__init__(switchboard, log_level)`, deferred `_initialize_ui` via `QTimer.singleShot(0)`, `add_reset_buttons`): Scope combo (Selected / All Transforms & Joints), Bake Options group (Sample By, Preserve Outside Keys, Optimize Keys, Bake Blend Shapes, Bake Inherited Visibility), Safety group (Use Override Layer — checked by default, disables Delete Inputs while on; Mute Drivers; Delete Inputs; Backup combo Auto/Always/Never mapping to `backup_file=None/True/False`), Bake + **Unbake** buttons, collapsible Output log (`TextEditLogHandler` redirect + `log_group` summaries), footer status/progress, header help via `fmt()`. Unbake wraps `SmartBake.restore()` (LIFO), self-enables/disables from `list_sessions()`, and deliberately stays clickable for a non-restorable (delete_inputs) session — the click surfaces the backup path and pops the dead entry so older sessions become reachable. Panel guards (self-critique pass): Selected scope + empty selection warns and bails instead of silently escalating to a whole-scene bake (the old tb020's `or None` fallback did exactly that), and all footer messaging is set *after* the progress context exits — `FooterProgressContext.__exit__` synchronously writes "Complete" to the status label, so text set inside the block gets clobbered. Tests: `test_smart_bake_slots.py` (10, real-Maya stub-UI pattern — scope/backup mapping, empty-selection bail, bake→footer/log/Unbake state, unbake round-trip through the panel, destructive-bake reporting) + `mock_tests/test_smart_bake_panel.py` (9, real offscreen Qt + mocked cmds through the real `MayaUiHandler` load path — .ui compiles, `SmartBakeSlots` (not the bare `SmartBake` engine) resolves, all referenced widgets exist, toggle wiring, combo items); real-Maya + real-Qt in one process crashes natively, hence the two-file split. Full affected suites green: smart_bake 57, smart_bake_slots 10, data_nodes 31, anim_utils 130, scene_exporter 74 (302/302 mayapy) + 9 pytest. Tentacle: `slots/maya/animation.py`'s `tb020` now opens the panel instead of baking inline (option-box config removed; the shared `animation.ui` widget is untouched — the Blender slots keep their inline option-box bake), tooltip updated via a minimal `tb020_init`; tentacle test_animation 9 + structural suites 22 green. **Can smart/sparse baking make the post-bake `optimize_keys` pass redundant? No — probed empirically in mayapy (2025):** `sparseAnimCurveBake=True` did nothing (10 dense keys on every channel, static ones included, base *and* layer mode); `bakeResults(smart=True)` is exact only when the driven motion is spline-representable between driver keys — perfect on a keyed-driver constraint (2 keys, 0.000000 deviation vs dense's 0.0019 sampling error) but **0.675 units off on a motion path** and approximate on expressions (sin wave, 0.0058) — and SmartBake's whole job is baking exactly those non-spline drivers (motion paths, expressions, IK). Even where smart wins, `optimize_keys` still owns two jobs bake flags can't do: deleting whole static curves (smart leaves 2-key constants on every never-moving channel) and the auto→fixed tangent freeze (FBX maps 'auto' to eTangentAuto, which corrupts sparse curves — long-established here). Dense bake + opt-in optimize stays.

- **2026-07-02 — SmartBake is now nondestructive by default, with an in-scene restore manifest: `SmartBake.restore()` reverses any bake — even in a later Maya session.** Every `bake()` (new flag `restorable=True`, default on) records a session manifest as JSON on the `data_internal` node (new `DataNodes.set_internal_string`/`get_internal_string`; internal node only — never `data_export`, whose attrs ride into the FBX). Restore data lives *in the scene*: anything bake mutates in place is **stashed** first — a disconnected `cmds.duplicate` of the animCurve (keys+tangents preserved), registered via a message connection on `data_internal` and `lockNode`-locked so *Optimize Scene Size* can't purge it; everything else is recorded as name+UUID refs (rename-safe) plus scalar state. `SmartBake.restore(session_id=None)` (LIFO; new module `bake_session.py` holds the store + engine (now at `anim_utils/smart_bake/bake_session.py`)) deletes the override layer, unmutes drivers to their **recorded** nodeState (previously `_mute_driver_nodes` didn't record prior state), re-enables IK, restores visibility, and for base-layer bakes deletes the baked curves, reconnects the snapshotted driver network (constraints/expressions/motion paths; unitConversions traced through — `connectAttr` recreates them), and unstashes SDK/blended key curves. Fixes proven-live destruction: **(1) IK permanently dead after every scene-exporter export** — after a layer bake with `disableImplicitControl=True`, `ikBlend` sat at 0 and the exporter's cleanup only deleted the layer (mayapy-proven: joints frozen at zero post-export); the manifest records/restores `ikBlend`, and the exporter's `finally` now calls `SmartBake.restore()` (legacy layer-delete kept as fallback). *Root cause pinned later the same day (see the panel entry above): the zeroing came from SmartBake pre-registering the bake attributes onto the fresh layer, not from DIC-on-a-layer per se — with the empty-layer fix, `ikBlend` stays 1.0 through a layer bake; the manifest record is retained as insurance for base-layer bakes, where DIC genuinely disables the handle.* **(2) Inherited-visibility bake hijacked the child's own vis curve** — `setKeyframe` merges baked keys into the existing curve, and the documented cleanup ("delete `result.visibility_curves`") would have destroyed the user's original animation; the original curve is now stashed pre-key and restored exactly (regression-pinned: 2 keys in → bake merges → restore → exactly the original 2 keys). **(3) Base-layer SDK conversion is unrecoverable** — `bakeResults` *deletes* the `animCurveU` node and creates a same-named `animCurveT` (UUID-proven), so nothing can be recovered after the fact; pre-bake stash + driver-connection record now round-trips it (SDK evaluates again post-restore). **Default flip:** `use_override_layer` now defaults **True** (base-layer mode remains available explicitly; tentacle Smart Bake option-box checkbox default updated to match); `backup_file` default changed `False`→`None` = auto-backup only on the one non-restorable path (`delete_inputs=True` in base mode, which flags its session non-restorable and points restore() at the backup). New: `SmartBake.session(**kwargs)` context manager (bake on enter, restore on exit — made for export workflows), `SmartBake.list_sessions()`, `BakeResult.session_id`, `RestoreResult` (exposed as `mtk.RestoreResult`; stale `mtk.smart_bake` include entry removed — the symbol never existed). Known v1 limitation (warned, documented): a bake-deleted intermediate blend node (e.g. pairBlend) can't be rebuilt — the stashed curve is reconnected directly to the channel where possible. Restore never raises on missing nodes; per-item issues collect in `RestoreResult.warnings`. Tests: 15 new (`TestNondestructiveRestore` — vis hijack, SDK/constraint/blendshape-alias base-layer round-trips, IK-after-layer regression, mute prior-state, LIFO, save/reopen cross-session restore, non-restorable delete_inputs, hand-deleted-layer robustness, context manager) + 6 `DataNodes` internal-string tests; 3 existing base-layer tests updated to request `use_override_layer=False` explicitly; affected suites green in fresh mayapy: smart_bake 56, data_nodes 31, anim_utils 130, scene_exporter 74 (291/291). Self-critique hardening: `restore()` peeks and pops only after the restore pass completes (an unexpected mid-restore failure stays retryable); the exporter's finally-block restore is exception-guarded so it can never mask an export error (layer-delete fallback intact); `snapshot_connections` guards `attributeQuery(listParent)` against blendShape weight aliases (raised RuntimeError, regression-pinned); `unstash_curve` survives a rename into a no-longer-existing namespace; `mute_drivers=True` + `delete_inputs=True` no longer mis-flags the session non-restorable (mute wins at cleanup, drivers survive). Also hardened the pre-existing `check_context_budget.py` crash (rglob died mid-traversal on a broken cloud-VFS placeholder; now an error-tolerant os.walk) and updated blendertk's PARITY_BACKLOG unbake_keys entry (nothing to port). Also deleted the confirmed-dead `anim_utils/unbake_keys.py` + its test (PyMel-style methods on cmds strings — all three functions removed 0 keys, superseded by `AnimUtils.optimize_keys`; flagged in the 2026-07-02 audit below) and the tentacle docstring reference to it.

- **2026-07-02 — anim_utils audit: ~40 verified bugs fixed across the subpackage (multi-agent audit, 3-lens adversarial verification + live-Maya repros).** Highlights, worst first. **Data loss**: `BlendshapeAnimator.finalize_for_export` deleted the weight animCurve (and would take skinClusters/joints on skinned meshes) via type-filtered `cmds.delete(listHistory(...))` while reporting "EXPORT READY" — proven live (weight keys 2→0); now uses `cmds.bakePartialHistory(prePostDeformers=True)`, the canonical non-deformer history bake. `AnimUtils.delete_keys(time=<float>)` wiped the ENTIRE timeline (`parse_time_range` only accepted `int`; a float fell through to None = "all"). `get_redundant_flat_keys(remove=True)` bypassed the undo queue entirely (om2 `MDGModifier` rebuild inside a `@CoreUtils.undoable` chunk) — Ctrl+Z restored nothing; rewritten as pure-cmds per-segment `cutKey` + batched auto→fixed tangent freezing (FBX-safe contract kept, lock-dance dropped — curve-node edits are lock-independent, which also stops stamping spurious own-lock flags on children of locked compounds). **Maya hang**: `SegmentKeys.execute_stagger(use_intervals=True, avoid_overlap=True)` with `spacing<=0` (the default!) spun forever; overlap now resolves arithmetically and non-positive spacing butts groups end-to-end. **Wrong-keys-moved family**: `move_keys_to_frame` and `align_selected_keyframes` moved keys with a `(min,max)` time-range edit, dragging UNSELECTED keys lying inside the span (mayapy-verified); both now shift exactly the selected times via new collision-safe `AnimUtils._shift_key_times` (`option="over"` so keys can pass unmoved neighbors). `move_keys_to_frame` also crashed on the PyMel leftover `connections[0].attrName()` (selected+channel-box combo) and on `None` from per-object selected-key queries (3 sites). `_resolve_keys` "selected" no longer falls back to unrelated objects' curves when the given objects own none. **ScaleKeys**: empty channel-box with `channel_box_attrs_only=True` silently scaled ALL attributes (now warns + no-ops); boundary epsilon 0.5 → 1e-3 (keys half a frame outside the range were scaled); invalid `group_mode` now warns-and-falls-back at the entry point instead of raising past a dead fallback branch; speed-mode report finally shows real `objects processed` and the uniform report its pivot; `_warn_no_targets` diagnostics actually populated (unfiltered probe). **Tangent correctness**: `_apply_curve_tangent_data` gated angle/weight per-side ('or' converted the stepped side of mixed keys to fixed, destroying holds); `_set_key_preserving_tangents` no longer overrides caller-passed tangent kwargs in its re-apply pass. **Shot tools**: shot_manifest Description edits corrupted the audio/narration field for audio steps; inline step rename now rejects duplicate step_ids (mirrors `parse_csv`); `active_shot_id` returned marker-time floats in markers mode; Maya **Redo** no longer pops the shot-state snapshot stack (was bound to the same pop-handler as Undo); ShotStore `set_object_hidden/pinned`, `remove_object_from_shots`, and all marker CRUD now `mark_dirty()` (edits were silently unpersisted); `save()` clears the dirty flag only after a successful backend write; `clear_active()` tears down the persistence backend's scene callbacks (a leaked kBeforeSave could write the old store into a new scene). **Behaviors/mapping**: fractional anchor 1.0 now truly equals `"end"` (offset sign was inverted); templates targeting `opacity` directly auto-create the attribute like `visibility` ones; `_audio_regex` no longer crashes on regex brace quantifiers (`str.format` → literal placeholder replace). **Misc**: `bake(only_keyed=True)` is now enforced in bake-all mode (was silently ignored) and the docstring no longer claims a playback-range default; `add_intermediate_keys` gained the missing `@CoreUtils.undoable` and restores the playhead; `SmartBake.get_time_range` floors/ceils instead of truncating fractional boundaries; playblast `_override_model_panels` guards `getPanel→None` and the Arnold `finally` no longer raises restoring an unset `imageFilePrefix`; `get_frame_ranges(precision=0)` no longer divides by zero; `set_keys_for_attributes` accepts float times; `Validator.validate_meshes` handles nonexistent nodes; blendshape `create()` coalesces `None` frames to class defaults (fixes `basic_workflow()`), resets state on failure (UI group-gating), `recover_with_targets` reports success for a repaired scene without tweens, un-applied tweens show "pending" (not green "Applied"), and multi-op slot/facade actions run in undo chunks. **Dead code deleted** (grep-confirmed unused repo-wide): `AnimUtils._apply_stagger/_shift_curves_by_amount/_calculate_speed_profile`, `ScaleKeys._execute_shift_operation/_filter_curves_by_channel_box` (divergent duplicate of the live SegmentKeys filter), shot_manifest plan machinery (`plan_object_keys`/`PlannedObject`/`PlannedKey`/`BuildPlan`/`build_plan` + `PlannedShot.planned_objects` — computed on every build, never consumed, with anchor semantics that diverged from `apply_to_shots`), `max_key_end`, sequencer `_get_composite_wav_path`/`_bound_audio_path`, ScaleKeys' dead selected-keys pre-check + unused imports; stagger verbose re-collect now uses the operative collection params. Tests: 17 new regression tests pinning the confirmed bugs (`TestAuditRegressionFixes` in test_anim_utils/test_scale_keys/test_segment_keys/test_blendshape_animator); full affected-suite run green in fresh Maya — 18 modules, 1,125 tests, 0 failures (anim_utils 130, scale_keys 66, segment_keys 43, stagger 20, smart_bake 41, playblast 14, blendshape 12, compute_plan 27, shot_plan 13, shot_manifest 224, +audio/behaviors/csv/mapping/export_view, sequencer 268, sequencer_controller 188, unbake 13). Known deliberate skip: `unbake_keys.py` is completely non-functional (PyMel-style curve methods on cmds strings — proven live, all three functions remove 0 keys while its 13 tests only cover pure helpers/no-op guards) — left untouched pending a decision on whether the module is still wanted.
- **2026-07-01 Fix — `invert_keys` Auto mode garbled the animation whenever the playhead sat inside the key range (the normal state).** Auto (time=None) placed a reversed *copy* at the current time: coincident keys were overwritten, non-coincident originals survived, and the result was neither the original nor a clean reversal — keys `(1,0)(4,3)(10,10)` with the playhead at 1 became `(1,10)(4,3)(7,3)(10,0)`. Auto now mirrors the keys **in place** (`t' = min + max − t`, `delete_original` implied — the existing insert-then-cut machinery becomes a move, which also sidesteps the cut-all-keys-deletes-the-curve gotcha), matching `btk.invert_keys`'s in-place mirror so the mtk↔btk behavior parity is real. Explicit `time` keeps the reversed-copy semantics (absolute/relative placement, optional `delete_original`) unchanged. Also added an `objects` first param (defaults to the current selection — consistent with the rest of `AnimUtils` and the btk signature; all existing callers use kwargs). Tentacle option-box tooltips (`s001`/`chk005`) updated to describe Auto as the in-place mirror. Tests: `test_invert_keys_auto_mirrors_in_place` (failed pre-fix) + five pinning the copy path, relative ping-pong, delete-original, vertical pivot flip, and the explicit-objects arg, replacing the old single `assertNotEqual` smoke test; full `test_anim_utils.py` suite 120 green under a fresh Maya instance.
